##### Penglai events
# by Silverswee(e)per

namespace = soh

# IDs 12801-13200 reserved



##### Penglai events

### Preparation events

# Start of event chain
character_event = {
	id = soh.12801
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12801
	picture = GFX_evt_chinese_mythology
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # prepare_penglai_expedition decision
	
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			which = penglai_preparation
			value = 0
		}
		
		clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
		
		any_realm_character = { # Expensive, but necessary
			clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12801 # Immortality will be mine!
		
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_playable = yes
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					AND = {
						NOR = {
							dynasty = ROOT
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
						}
						opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < -25
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				is_rival = ROOT
				is_powerful_vassal = yes
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				is_rival = ROOT
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				is_powerful_vassal = yes
			}
			
			clr_character_flag = penglai_rival_sabotage # In case they've done this before
			
			save_event_target_as = penglai_rival
		}
		
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				is_playable = yes
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12802 } # Inform vassals
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12803 days = 10 random = 10 } # Preparations
	}
}

# Inform vassals
character_event = {
	id = soh.12802
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12802
	picture = GFX_evt_chinese_mythology
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12802 # I wish them luck!
		
		ai_chance = {	
			factor = 100
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_friend = FROM
				is_lover = FROM
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12802 # May they live in interesting times!
		
		ai_chance = {	
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = FROM
					trait = cruel
					trait = deceitful
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = ambitious
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOT = {
					is_rival = FROM
				}
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value >= 25
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOT = {
					is_rival = FROM
				}
				opinion = {
					who = FROM
					value >= 50
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			character = event_target:penglai_rival
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12802 # Perhaps I should try to sabotage this...
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_rival_sabotage
		
		ai_chance = {	
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 100
				is_rival = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
}

# Hidden progress event to ensure the event chain cancels if it should
character_event = {
	id = soh.12803
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		is_eligible_for_penglai_trigger = yes
	}
	
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				could_have_chinese_government_trigger = no
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12804 } # Ineligible due to religion/culture
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				can_reach_penglai_trigger = no
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12806 } # Ineligible due to no longer having a naval path
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				lower_real_tier_than = duke
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12808 } # Ineligible due to no longer being a king+ ruler
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				in_revolt = yes
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12810 } # Ineligible due to rebelling against your rightful liege
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12812 } # Ineligible due to being unable to travel
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		change_variable = {
			which = penglai_preparation
			value = 1
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_equal = {
					which = penglai_preparation
					value = 1
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12814 } # Any specific preparations you'd like to make?
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_equal = {
					which = penglai_preparation
					value = 2
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12815 } # Anything specific you'd like to bring?
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_equal = {
					which = penglai_preparation
					value = 2
				}
				any_vassal = {
					is_adult = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					could_have_chinese_government_trigger = yes
					is_playable = yes
					war = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12816 } # Are you maybe sending a vassal in your place?
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12835 } # Time for departure
		}
	}
}

# Event chain cancelled - no longer eligible due to religion/culture
character_event = {
	id = soh.12804
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12804
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12804 # So be it!
		
		remove_trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
		
		clr_character_flag = launched_penglai_expedition
		
		clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
		
		any_realm_character = {
			clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
		}
		
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12805 } # Inform vassals
		}
	}
}

# Do you want to focus on anything special during the preparations?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12814
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12814
	picture = GFX_evt_treasure_fleet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12814 # Military preparations are paramount!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_military_preparations
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				has_education_martial_trigger = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = brave
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = strategist
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12814 # We'll need all the charts we can get!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				has_education_learning_trigger = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				is_dumb_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12814 # Additional ships would be most worthwhile!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_naval_preparations
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				has_education_martial_trigger = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_SOH_12814 # Knowledge about the Immortals and Penglai is necessary!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_legend_preparations
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				has_education_learning_trigger = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = erudite
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = scholar
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = mystic
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		character_event = { id = soh.12803 days = 10 random = 10 } # Preparations
	}
}

# Are you going yourself or sending a vassal?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12816
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12816
	picture = GFX_evt_treasure_fleet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				could_have_chinese_government_trigger = yes
				is_playable = yes
				war = no
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				is_powerful_vassal = yes
				OR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					is_rival = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_voter = yes
					dynasty = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				OR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					is_rival = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_voter = yes
					dynasty = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				is_powerful_vassal = yes
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = penglai_vassal
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12816 # I am going myself, of course!
		
		event_target:penglai_rival = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12832 days = 5 } # Sabotage possible
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = craven
					trait = slothful
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:penglai_vassal
						is_lover = event_target:penglai_vassal
						AND = {
							trait = trusting
							NOT = {
								is_rival = event_target:penglai_vassal
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = brave
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = diligent
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12816 # I will send a vassal if I can...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:penglai_vassal = {
					NOT = {
						character = event_target:penglai_rival
					}
				}
			}
			
			event_target:penglai_rival = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12832 days = 5 } # Sabotage possible
			}
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_vassal = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12817 } # Vassal asked to go
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:penglai_rival
					trait = paranoid
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = craven
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		character_event = { id = soh.12803 days = 10 random = 10 } # Preparations
	}
}

# Inform vassal - liege wants you to go
character_event = {
	id = soh.12817
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12817
	picture = GFX_evt_treasure_fleet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clear_flags_with_prefix = penglai_request_
		
		# Determine options; at most two aside from "Nothing" and "Refuse"
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = penglai_request_money
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					has_dlc = "Conclave"
					NOR = {
						holds_favor_on = FROM
						owes_favor_to = FROM
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = penglai_request_favor
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					has_dlc = "Conclave"
					holds_favor_on = FROM
				}
				set_character_flag = penglai_request_favor_repaid
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					FROM = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = penglai_request_grace
			}
		}
		
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_character_flag = penglai_request_money
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = penglai_request_money
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_character_flag = penglai_request_favor
					}
					has_dlc = "Conclave"
					NOR = {
						holds_favor_on = FROM
						owes_favor_to = FROM
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = penglai_request_favor
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = {

		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12819 # Very well!

			limit = {
				real_tier = emperor
			}

				to = FROM
				value = 0.2
				min = 20
				max = 100
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				real_tier = king
			}
			
			transfer_scaled_wealth = {
				to = FROM
				value = 0.15
				min = 15
				max = 75
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			transfer_scaled_wealth = {
				to = FROM
				value = 0.1
				min = 10
				max = 50
			}
		}

			character_event = { id = soh.12820 } # Inform FROM
			
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_going_to_penglai_for_you
				months = 12
			}
		}

			factor = 100

						}
					}
					trait = greedy

				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_lover = FROM
			}
		}

			trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
			reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_going_to_penglai_for_you
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		any_opinion_modifier_target = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_going_to_penglai_for_you
				}
			}
			
			liege = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12834 } # Inform liege
			}
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_vassal = {
						trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12836 } # Ruler departs
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal = {
					trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12838 } # Ruler needs to go
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12841 } # Leader departs
		}
	}
}

# Voyage setup
character_event = {
	id = soh.12844
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = penglai_expedition_go_for_liege
			}
			
			FROMFROM = {
				save_event_target_as = penglai_sender
			}
			
			copy_penglai_flags_effect = yes
		}
		
		else = {
			save_event_target_as = penglai_sender
		}
		
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_fruitless
			}
			
			75 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.8
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_volcanic_island
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					NOR = {
						has_global_flag = aztec_explorers
						has_global_flag = demon_child_to_aztecs
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_pacific_return
						has_global_flag = china_knows_of_burning_empire
						has_global_flag = lost_merchant_went_east
						has_global_flag = sunrise_invasion_happened
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_will_arrive_at_nazca
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_at_nazca
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_aztecs
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_will_arrive_at_yamatai
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_at_yamatai
					}
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								ROOT = {
									ai = no
								}
								event_target:penglai_sender = {
									ai = no
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								has_game_rule = {
									name = supernatural_events
									value = off
								}
							}
						}
						has_game_rule = {
							name = supernatural_events
							value = unrestricted
						}
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
				
				set_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					NOR = {
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_will_arrive_at_atlantis
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_at_atlantis
						has_global_flag = atlantis_lost
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
				
				set_global_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
			}
			
			5 = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								ROOT = {
									ai = no
								}
								event_target:penglai_sender = {
									ai = no
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								has_game_rule = {
									name = supernatural_events
									value = off
								}
							}
						}
						has_game_rule = {
							name = supernatural_events
							value = unrestricted
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_horai
			}
			
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_will_arrive_at_penglai
						has_global_flag = treasure_fleet_at_penglai
					}
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								ROOT = {
									ai = no
								}
								event_target:penglai_sender = {
									ai = no
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								has_game_rule = {
									name = supernatural_events
									value = off
								}
							}
						}
						has_game_rule = {
							name = supernatural_events
							value = unrestricted
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
				
				set_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
			}
		}
		
		# Intermediate step
		random_list = {
			33 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_storm
			}
			
			33 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				set_character_flag = penglai_destination_sharks
			}
			
			34 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					has_character_flag = penglai_charts_preparations
				}
				
				# No intermediate step
			}
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12845 days = 30 random = 60 } # Flavor selection
	}
}

	}

					trait = sex_interest_female
				}

					age = 19
					female = yes

					religion = event_target:penglai_sender
					trait = lustful

					trait = ambitious
					trait = underhanded_rogue
				}
			}

				trigger = {
					trait = sex_interest_male
				}
				
				create_character = {
					age = 19
					female = no
					culture = event_target:penglai_sender
					religion = event_target:penglai_sender
					trait = lustful
					trait = strong
					trait = ambitious
					trait = underhanded_rogue
				}
			}
		}
		
		new_character = {
			save_event_target_as = sex_partner
			
			randomize_event_target_sexuality_effect = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12847 # I'll proposition them!
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12848 } # Target propositioned
		}

			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = chaste
					AND = {
						trait = celibate
						NOT = {
							trait = lustful
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = humble
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12847 # I don't think so!
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			leave_court_and_die_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			trigger = {

					trait = lustful

						trait = celibate
						trait = proud
					}
				}
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1

					trait = chaste

			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = slothful
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = erudite
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = scholar
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = stressed
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12851 # I want to relax!
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			character_event = { id = soh.12859 days = 7 random = 7 } # Relaxing is nice
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = diligent
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Assassination attempt - died
character_event = {
	id = soh.12863
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			character = event_target:penglai_sender
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12863
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12863
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12863 # So it ends...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:penglai_sender
				}
			}
			
			event_target:penglai_sender = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12864 } # Inform sender
				
				any_realm_lord = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						NOR = {
							character = event_target:penglai_rival
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12865 } # Inform vassals
				}
				
				event_target:penglai_rival = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12866 } # Inform sender of assassin
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					ai = no
					NOR = {
						character = event_target:penglai_rival
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12867 } # Inform vassals
			}
			
			event_target:penglai_rival = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12868 } # Inform sender of assassin
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_expedition_event_chain_death
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_expedition_ongoing
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
		
		top_liege = {
			clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			
			any_realm_character = {
				clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			}
		}
			
		death = {
			death_reason = death_murder_unknown
			killer = event_target:penglai_rival
		}
	}
}

			}

	}

			add_trait_partially_ambitious_effect = yes
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait_partially_brave_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = craven
					NOR = {
						is_friend = event_target:penglai_sender
						is_lover = event_target:penglai_sender
						character = event_target:penglai_sender
						AND = {
							is_rival = event_target:penglai_sender
							trait = ambitious
						}
					}
				}
			}

				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}

				trait = stubborn
			}
		}

		}

			factor = 100

				}
			}

				factor = 10
				trait = content

			}
		}
	}
}

# Destination setup - make sure the event chain should keep on going
character_event = {
	id = soh.12872
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		trait = on_penglai_expedition
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = penglai_interrupt
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = penglai_destination_storm
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12874 } # Storm ahead
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = penglai_destination_sharks
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12881 } # Shark-infested waters ahead
		}
		
		else = {
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				
				penglai_destination_fruitless = { character_event = { id = soh.12873 } } # Fruitless search
				penglai_destination_volcanic_island = { narrative_event = { id = soh.12888 } } # Volcanic island
				penglai_destination_yamatai = { narrative_event = { id = soh.12903 } } # Yamatai
				penglai_destination_atlantis = { character_event = { id = soh.12925 } } # Atlantis
				penglai_destination_aztecs = { character_event = { id = soh.12937 } } # Aztecs
				penglai_destination_horai = { narrative_event = { id = soh.12947 } } # Horai
				penglai_destination_penglai = { character_event = { id = soh.12959 } } # Penglai
			}
		}
	}
}

### Destination events - storm

# There is a great storm ahead - press on or turn back?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12874
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			character = event_target:penglai_sender
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12874
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12874
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_kraken
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12874 # We press on!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						has_character_flag = penglai_naval_preparations
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12875 days = 2 random = 5 } # Fleet destroyed
				}
				
				34 = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12879 days = 2 random = 5 } # Fleet made it through the storm
				}
				
				33 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						has_character_flag = penglai_naval_preparations
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12880 days = 2 random = 5 } # Fleet takes too much damage to continue
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:penglai_sender
					trait = craven
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_character_flag = penglai_naval_preparations
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = brave
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
				OR = {
					character = event_target:penglai_sender
					is_rival = event_target:penglai_sender
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12874 # We must head back...
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_return_storm
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:penglai_sender
						is_lover = event_target:penglai_sender
						character = event_target:penglai_sender
					}
					trait = brave
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = craven
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}

				}
			}

				character_event = { id = soh.12876 } # Inform sender

					limit = {
						ai = no
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12877 } # Inform vassals of sender
				}
			}
		}

			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					ai = no
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12878 } # Inform vassals
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_expedition_event_chain_death
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_expedition_ongoing
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai

		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
		
		top_liege = {

			any_realm_character = {
				clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			}
		}

		}
	}

# The waters are extremely shark-infested - press on or turn back?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12881
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			character = event_target:penglai_sender
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12881
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12881
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_kraken
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12881 # We press on!
		
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						has_character_flag = penglai_naval_preparations
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						has_character_flag = penglai_military_preparations
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12882 days = 2 random = 5 } # Eaten by sharks
				}
				
				34 = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12886 days = 2 random = 5 } # Fleet can continue
				}
				
				33 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						has_character_flag = penglai_naval_preparations
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						has_character_flag = penglai_military_preparations
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12887 days = 2 random = 5 } # Too many sailors lost to sharks
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:penglai_sender
					trait = craven
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_character_flag = penglai_military_preparations
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = brave
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
				OR = {
					character = event_target:penglai_sender
					is_rival = event_target:penglai_sender
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12881 # We must head back...
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_return_sharks
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:penglai_sender
						is_lover = event_target:penglai_sender
						character = event_target:penglai_sender
					}
					trait = brave
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = craven
			}
		}
	}
}

		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12905
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12905
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_china_civil_war
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12905 # Not possible!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:penglai_sender
				}
			}
			
			event_target:penglai_sender = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12906 } # Inform sender
				
				any_realm_lord = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12907 } # Inform vassals of sender
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					ai = no
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12908 } # Inform vassals
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_expedition_event_chain_death
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_expedition_ongoing
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
		
		top_liege = {
			clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			
			any_realm_character = {
				clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_battle
		}
	}
}

# Offer accepted - departure with servant for leader or leader's liege
character_event = {
	id = soh.12930
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			character = event_target:penglai_sender
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12930
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12930
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_treasure_fleet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		create_random_soldier = {
			age = 25
			female = 50
			culture = atlantean
			religion = hellenic_pagan
			dynasty = random
			trait = skilled_tactician
			trait = ambitious
			trait = deceitful
			trait = patient
			random_traits = yes
		}
			
		new_character = {
			save_event_target_as = atlantean
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_character_flag = penglai_expedition_go_myself
					}
				}
				
				opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_loyal_servant
					years = 100
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:atlantean = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12931 days = 365 random = 730  } # "Loyal" servant isn't actually
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12930 # Time to head home!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_return_atlantis_servant
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
	}
}

# "Loyal" servant stops being loyal
character_event = {
	id = soh.12931
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12931
	picture = GFX_evt_treasure_fleet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		any_opinion_modifier_target = {
			reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_loyal_servant
			}
			NOR = {
				is_friend = ROOT
				is_lover = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12931 # My only loyalty is to Atlantis!
		
		any_opinion_modifier_target = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_loyal_servant
				}
				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			ROOT = {
				remove_opinion = {
					who = PREV
					modifier = opinion_loyal_servant
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = soh.12933
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			character = event_target:penglai_sender
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12933
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12933
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12933 # No!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:penglai_sender
				}
			}
			
			event_target:penglai_sender = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12934 } # Inform sender
				
				any_realm_lord = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12935 } # Inform vassals of sender
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					ai = no
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12936 } # Inform vassals
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_expedition_event_chain_death
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_expedition_ongoing
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
		
		top_liege = {
			clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			
			any_realm_character = {
				clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_murder_unknown
		}
	}
}

			character = event_target:penglai_sender
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12953
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {

			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12953
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12953 # No! NO! NOOOOO!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:penglai_sender
				}
			}
			
			event_target:penglai_sender = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12954 } # Inform sender
				
				any_realm_lord = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12955 } # Inform vassals of sender
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					ai = no
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12956 } # Inform vassals
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_expedition_event_chain_death
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_expedition_ongoing
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
		
		top_liege = {
			clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			
			any_realm_character = {
				clean_up_penglai_character_flags_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_spirits
		}
	}
}

		}
		
		else = {
			prestige = -500
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
	}
}

# Got Peach
character_event = {
	id = soh.12968
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
				has_character_flag = penglai_eat_peach
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12968
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
				has_character_flag = penglai_eat_peach
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12968
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_chinese_mythology
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = penglai_eat_peach
			OR = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
				event_target:penglai_sender = {
					is_alive = no
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12968 # I knew my quest would succeed!
		
		add_trait_immortal_effect = yes
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_return_penglai_immortal
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = penglai_eat_peach
			NOT = {
				character = event_target:penglai_sender
			}
			event_target:penglai_sender = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12968 # My liege never needs to find out!
		
		add_trait_immortal_effect = yes
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_return_penglai_traitor
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = penglai_eat_peach
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12968 # We must return with this at once!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_has_peach
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_return_penglai_peach
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12970 days = 30 random = 30 } # Return setup
	}
}

	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_alive = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12977 # A commendable initiative!
		
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = soh.12978 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_penglai_scheme_support
			months = 12
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:penglai_liege
					is_lover = event_target:penglai_liege
					AND = {
						OR = {
							dynasty = event_target:penglai_liege
							is_close_relative = event_target:penglai_liege
						}
						NOT = {
							is_rival = event_target:penglai_liege
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:penglai_liege
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_lover = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12977 # I want nothing to do with this!
		
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = soh.12979 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				dynasty = event_target:penglai_liege
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = craven
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
}

# Returned - headed back early
character_event = {
	id = soh.12980
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12980
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12980 # I am a failure!
		
		prestige = -1000
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				government = chinese_imperial_government
			}
			mandate_penalty_medium_effect = yes
		}
		
		set_council_malcontent = yes
		
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				NAND = {
					character = event_target:penglai_rival
					has_character_flag = penglai_faction_scheme
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12981 } # Inform vassals
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_rival = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_liege = {
						character = ROOT
						is_landed = yes
					}
					has_character_flag = penglai_faction_scheme
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						trait = treasure_fleet_preparation
						trait = treasure_fleet_voyage
					}
					has_actual_regency_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12982 } # Inform schemer
		}
	}
}

	id = soh.12982
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12982
	picture = GFX_evt_china_unrest
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12982 # Time to strike!
		
		event_target:penglai_sender = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					higher_real_tier_than = baron
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						trait = treasure_fleet_preparation
						trait = treasure_fleet_voyage
					}
					has_actual_regency_trigger = no
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_penglai_scheme_support
					}
				}
			}
			
			letter_event = { id = soh.13126 } # Called on to support the faction
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.13127 days = 2 } # Time for the ultimatum
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12982 # I don't have enough support...
		
		prestige = -100
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12999
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12999 # Their reign will not be eternal, then...
	}
}

# Scheming vassal might put scheme in motion
character_event = {
	id = soh.13000
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13000
	picture = GFX_evt_china_unrest
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_13000 # Time to strike!
		
		event_target:penglai_sender = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					higher_real_tier_than = baron
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						trait = treasure_fleet_preparation
						trait = treasure_fleet_voyage
					}
					has_actual_regency_trigger = no
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_penglai_scheme_support
					}
				}
			}
			
			letter_event = { id = soh.13126 } # Called on to support the faction
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.13127 days = 2 } # Time for the ultimatum
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_13000 # I don't have enough support...
		
		prestige = -100
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

# Returned - Yamatai (non-Himiko)
character_event = {
	id = soh.13001
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13001
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_13001 # I am a failure!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				government = chinese_imperial_government
			}
			mandate_penalty_medium_effect = yes
		}
		
		set_council_malcontent = yes
		
		prestige = -100
		
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				NAND = {
					character = event_target:penglai_rival
					has_character_flag = penglai_faction_scheme
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.13002 } # Inform vassals
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_rival = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_liege = {
						character = ROOT
						is_landed = yes
					}
					has_character_flag = penglai_faction_scheme
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						trait = treasure_fleet_preparation
						trait = treasure_fleet_voyage
					}
					has_actual_regency_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.13003 } # Inform schemer
		}
	}
}

# Inform vassals (not schemer)

				government = chinese_imperial_government
			}
			mandate_penalty_medium_effect = yes
		}
		
		set_council_malcontent = yes
		
		prestige = -100
		
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				NAND = {
					character = event_target:penglai_rival
					has_character_flag = penglai_faction_scheme
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.13023 } # Inform vassals
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_rival = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_liege = {
						character = ROOT
						is_landed = yes
					}
					has_character_flag = penglai_faction_scheme
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						trait = treasure_fleet_preparation
						trait = treasure_fleet_voyage
					}
					has_actual_regency_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.13024 } # Inform schemer
		}
	}
}

					character = event_target:penglai_rival

			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.13044 } # Inform vassals
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_rival = {
			character_event = { id = soh.13045 } # Inform schemer
		}
	}

# Returned - headed back early
character_event = {
	id = soh.13049
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13049
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_13049 # I have failed!
		
		prestige = -100
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							character = event_target:penglai_sender
							AND = {
								is_close_relative = event_target:penglai_sender
								dynasty = event_target:penglai_sender
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:penglai_sender = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				detract_grace_minor_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				detract_grace_trivial_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_sender = {
			character_event = { id = soh.13050 } # Inform sender
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13125
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_13125 # Really?
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_did_not_reward_me
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					#is_rival = FROM
				}
			}
			
			add_rival_single_character_effect = yes
		}
		
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_friend = FROM
				}
				
				remove_friend_single_character_effect = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_lover = FROM
				}
				
				remove_lover = FROM
			}
		}
	}
}



### Scheme - liege is a failure

# Vassal backing scheme informed - scheme in motion
letter_event = {
	id = soh.13126
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13126
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			any_liege = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = PREV
					modifier = opinion_penglai_failure
				}
			}
		}
		any_liege = {
			reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = FROM
				modifier = opinion_penglai_failure
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_13126 # We will not remain subjects of this failure!
		
		set_character_flag = penglai_failure_revolt
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					any_liege = {
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = FROM
							modifier = opinion_penglai_failure
						}
						OR = {
							is_lover = ROOT
							is_friend = ROOT
							AND = {
								OR = {
									dynasty = ROOT
									is_close_relative = ROOT
								}
								NOT = {
									is_rival = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_13126 # I think not!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				any_liege = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						modifier = opinion_penglai_failure
					}
					OR = {
						is_lover = ROOT
						is_friend = ROOT
						AND = {
							OR = {
								dynasty = ROOT
								is_close_relative = ROOT
							}
							NOT = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Final setup for the faction leader
character_event = {
	id = soh.13127
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		any_liege = {
			reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_penglai_failure
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = penglai_failure_revolt
		
		any_liege = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_penglai_failure
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = penglai_sender
			
			ROOT = {
				set_defacto_liege = PREV
			}
			
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = penglai_failure_revolt
				}
				
				set_defacto_liege = PREV
			}
		}
		
		start_faction = {
			faction = faction_penglai_independence
		}
		
		event_target:penglai_sender = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						character = ROOT
						has_character_flag = penglai_failure_revolt
					}
				}
				
				join_faction = {
					faction = faction_penglai_independence
					faction_leader = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.13128 } # Inform faction leader
	}
}

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

			letter_event = { id = soh.13129 } # Liege gets ultimatum
		}
	}
}

# Ultimatum - Independence
letter_event = {
	id = soh.13129
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13129

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_penglai_independence }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {

	id = soh.13130
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_13130

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		any_demesne_title = {
			limit = {
				controls_religion = no
				higher_real_tier_than = BARON

				FROM = {
					NAND = { # If the old liege can use the subjugation CB, give no claims
						religion_group = pagan_group
						culture_group = ROOT
					}
				}
			}

			add_weak_pressed_claim = FROM
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EXCELLENT

		clr_character_flag = faction_independence_ultimatum_taken
	}
}

### Misc fail states

# Death of ruler mid-preparation
character_event = {
	id = soh.13134
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	
	trigger = {
		trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
		NOT = {
			any_opinion_modifier_target = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_going_to_penglai_for_you
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					ai = no
					trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = soh.13135 days = 1 } # Inform vassals
		}
	}
}

# Cleanup of global flag in case something has broken
province_event = {
	id = soh.13144
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # prepare_penglai_expedition decision
	
	immediate = {
		on_penglai_expedition = {
			remove_trait = on_penglai_expedition
		}
		
		penglai_expedition_preparation = {
			remove_trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
		}
		
		penglai_expedition_preparation = { # Needed in case there are two eligible characters!
			remove_trait = penglai_expedition_preparation
		}
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_expedition_ongoing
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_yamatai
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_atlantis
		
		clr_global_flag = penglai_destination_penglai
	}
}
